Bible Quiz Questions – Old Testament – Volume 2: Historical Books I
Joshua–2 Kings · 151 chapters · 1208 questions
Collection index and Volume 1
Each chapter has eight questions: three Easy, three Medium and two Hard. The question order and multiple-choice answer positions are mixed. Difficulty is editorial: Hard questions require closer recall, comparison of passages or careful interpretation.
Quotation questions marked WEB use the World English Bible. Other questions preserve the study material’s wording or paraphrase a passage. Accept a clearly equivalent answer when exact wording is not being tested.
Read the passage in context before applying it today or connecting it to Christ. A character’s statement is not automatically the narrator’s conclusion. Theological questions use the course summaries listed below alongside Scripture; disputed readings are identified in the explanations.
Sources: Old testament course summary · Gospel Worldview course summary · World English Bible
Joshua
Joshua 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… to the great river, the river Euphrates, all the land of the ______, and to the great sea toward the going down of the sun, shall …”? (1:4, WEB) Easy
A) officers B) Hittites C) courageous D) prosperous
Answer: B. Hittites. Joshua 1:4 uses Hittites: “… to the great river, the river Euphrates, all the land of the Hittites, and to the great sea toward the going down of the sun, shall …”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Your wives, your little ones, and your cattle, shall ______ in the land which Moses gave you beyond the Jordan; but you shall pass over before …” (1:14); “until Yahweh have given your brothers ______, as he has given you, and they also have possessed the land which Yahweh your God gives them: then …” (1:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: abide; rest. The first passage reads “Your wives, your little ones, and your cattle, shall abide in the land which Moses gave you beyond the Jordan; but you shall pass over before …”; the second reads “until Yahweh have given your brothers rest, as he has given you, and they also have possessed the land which Yahweh your God gives them: then …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. True or false: The LORD told Joshua to be strong and courageous. (1:6-9) Easy
Answer: True. Joshua 1:6 records: “Be strong and of good courage; for you shall cause this people to inherit the land which I swore to their fathers to give them.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… of the camp, and command the people, saying, Prepare food; for within ______ days you are to pass over this Jordan, to go in to possess …”? (1:11, WEB) Medium
A) three B) seventeen C) thousand D) fourth
Answer: A. three. The passage specifies three: “… of the camp, and command the people, saying, Prepare food; for within three days you are to pass over this Jordan, to go in to possess …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “To the Reubenites, and to the Gadites, and to the half-tribe of ______, spoke Joshua, saying,” (1:12); “Remember the word which Moses the ______ of Yahweh commanded you, saying, Yahweh your God gives you rest, and will give you this land.” (1:13, WEB) Hard
A) Manasseh; judge B) Eliphaz; servant C) Manasseh; servant D) servant; Manasseh
Answer: C. Manasseh; servant. Verse 12 reads “To the Reubenites, and to the Gadites, and to the half-tribe of Manasseh, spoke Joshua, saying,”; verse 13 reads “Remember the word which Moses the servant of Yahweh commanded you, saying, Yahweh your God gives you rest, and will give you this land.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… able to stand before you all the days of your ______. As I was with ______, so I will be with you; I will not fail …” (1:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: life; Moses. The wording is “… able to stand before you all the days of your life. As I was with Moses, so I will be with you; I will not fail …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Then Daniel commanded the officers of the people, saying,” (1:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Then Joshua commanded the officers of the people, saying,” It says Joshua, not Daniel.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Moses my servant is dead; now therefore arise, go over this ______, you, and all this people, to the land which I do give to them, …” (1:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jordan. The cited words are “Moses my servant is dead; now therefore arise, go over this Jordan, you, and all this people, to the land which I do give to them, …”
Joshua 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “It was told the king of ______, saying, Behold, there came men in here tonight of the children of Israel to search out the land.” (2:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jericho. The cited words are “It was told the king of Jericho, saying, Behold, there came men in here tonight of the children of Israel to search out the land.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… you came out of Egypt; and what you did to the two ______ of the Amorites, who were beyond the Jordan, to Sihon and to Og, …”? (2:10, WEB) Medium
A) rulers B) soldiers C) kings D) elder
Answer: C. kings. The passage specifies kings: “… you came out of Egypt; and what you did to the two kings of the Amorites, who were beyond the Jordan, to Sihon and to Og, …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… pursued after them the way to the ______ to the fords: and as soon as those who ______ after them were gone out, they shut the …” (2:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Jordan; pursued. The wording is “… pursued after them the way to the Jordan to the fords: and as soon as those who pursued after them were gone out, they shut the …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Now therefore, spies swear to me by Yahweh, since I have dealt kindly with you, that you also will deal kindly with my father's house, and …” (2:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Now therefore, please swear to me by Yahweh, since I have dealt kindly with you, that you also will deal kindly with my father's house, and …” It says please, not spies.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, rahab hid the two spies on the ______ of her house. (2:6) Easy
Answer: roof. Joshua 2:6 records: “But she had brought them up to the roof, and hid them with the stalks of flax, which she had laid in order on the roof.”
Q6. Multiple choice: Why does Rahab's confession matter alongside her protection of the spies? (2:8-14) Hard
A) She asks Israel to worship her. B) She recognizes the LORD's authority and seeks refuge for her household. C) She claims that Jericho's gods defeated the LORD. D) She says no rescue is needed.
Answer: B. She recognizes the LORD's authority and seeks refuge for her household.. Rahab is an outsider who responds to what she has heard of God. The course uses her story to show that ethnicity alone does not exclude someone who turns toward God.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The men said to her, Our ______ for yours, if you don't utter this our business; and it shall be, when Yahweh gives us the land, …” (2:14); “She said to them, Get you to the mountain, lest the pursuers ______ on you; and hide yourselves there three days, until the pursuers be returned: …” (2:16, WEB) Hard
A) life; light B) life; fear C) refuge; light D) light; life
Answer: A. life; light. Verse 14 reads “The men said to her, Our life for yours, if you don't utter this our business; and it shall be, when Yahweh gives us the land, …”; verse 16 reads “She said to them, Get you to the mountain, lest the pursuers light on you; and hide yourselves there three days, until the pursuers be returned: …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… men went I don't know: pursue after them quickly; for you will ______ them.”? (2:5, WEB) Easy
A) overtake B) above C) beneath D) Truly
Answer: A. overtake. Joshua 2:5 uses overtake: “… men went I don't know: pursue after them quickly; for you will overtake them.”
Joshua 3
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… in the morning; and they removed from Shittim, and came to the ______, he and all the children of Israel; and they lodged there before they …” (3:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jordan. The cited words are “… in the morning; and they removed from Shittim, and came to the Jordan, he and all the children of Israel; and they lodged there before they …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the ark of the covenant of the Lord of all the earth ______ over before you into the Jordan.” (3:11); “… bear the ark of Yahweh, the Lord of all the earth, shall ______ in the waters of the Jordan, that the waters of the Jordan shall …” (3:13, WEB) Hard
A) cubits; rest B) passes; rest C) rest; passes D) passes; refuge
Answer: B. passes; rest. Verse 11 reads “… the ark of the covenant of the Lord of all the earth passes over before you into the Jordan.”; verse 13 reads “… bear the ark of Yahweh, the Lord of all the earth, shall rest in the waters of the Jordan, that the waters of the Jordan shall …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “You shall command the priests who ______ the ark of the covenant, saying, When you are come to the brink of the waters of the Jordan, …”? (3:8, WEB) Medium
A) goats B) bull C) bear D) camels
Answer: C. bear. The passage specifies bear: “You shall command the priests who bear the ark of the covenant, saying, When you are come to the brink of the waters of the Jordan, …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… spoke to the ______, saying, Take up the ark of the covenant, and pass over before the people. They took up the ______ of the covenant, …” (3:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: priests; ark. The wording is “… spoke to the priests, saying, Take up the ark of the covenant, and pass over before the people. They took up the ark of the covenant, …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. True or false: The priests carried the ark into the Jordan River. (3:14-15) Easy
Answer: True. Joshua 3:14 records: “It happened, when the people removed from their tents, to pass over the Jordan, the priests who bore the ark of the covenant being before the people;”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Joshua said, Hereby you shall know that the living God is among you, and that he will without fail drive out from before you the Canaanite, …” (3:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Joshua said, Hereby you shall know that the living God is among you, and that he will without fail drive out from before you the Canaanite, …”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Jordan; and all Israel passed over on dry ground, until all the ______ were passed clean over the Jordan.” (3:17); “… Sea, were wholly cut off: and the people passed over right against ______.” (3:16, WEB) Hard
Answer: nation; Jericho. The first passage reads “… Jordan; and all Israel passed over on dry ground, until all the nation were passed clean over the Jordan.”; the second reads “… Sea, were wholly cut off: and the people passed over right against Jericho.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… they commanded the people, saying, When you see the ark of the ______ of Yahweh your God, and the priests the Levites bearing it, then you …”? (3:3, WEB) Easy
A) covenant B) testimonies C) statutes D) commandments
Answer: A. covenant. Joshua 3:3 uses covenant: “… they commanded the people, saying, When you see the ark of the covenant of Yahweh your God, and the priests the Levites bearing it, then you …”
Joshua 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… the first month, and encamped in Gilgal, on the east border of ______.” (4:19); “He spoke to the children of ______, saying, When your children shall ask their fathers in time to come, saying, What mean these stones?” (4:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: Jericho; Israel. The first passage reads “… the first month, and encamped in Gilgal, on the east border of Jericho.”; the second reads “He spoke to the children of Israel, saying, When your children shall ask their fathers in time to come, saying, What mean these stones?”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… Because the waters of the Jordan were cut off before the ______ of the ______ of Yahweh; when it passed over the Jordan, the waters of …” (4:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: ark; covenant. The wording is “… Because the waters of the Jordan were cut off before the ark of the covenant of Yahweh; when it passed over the Jordan, the waters of …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and ______ said to them, Pass over before the ark of Yahweh your God into the midst of the Jordan, and take up every man of …”? (4:5, WEB) Easy
A) Belshazzar B) Joshua C) Josiah D) Levi
Answer: B. Joshua. Joshua 4:5 uses Joshua: “and Joshua said to them, Pass over before the ark of Yahweh your God into the midst of the Jordan, and take up every man of …”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The children of Reuben, and the children of Aaron, and the half-tribe of Manasseh, passed over armed before the children of Israel, as Moses spoke to …” (4:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The children of Reuben, and the children of Gad, and the half-tribe of Manasseh, passed over armed before the children of Israel, as Moses spoke to …” It says Gad, not Aaron.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… they feared him, as they feared Moses, all the days of his ______.” (4:14); “… of the Jordan returned to their place, and went over all its ______, as before.” (4:18, WEB) Hard
A) life; banks B) life; days C) salvation; banks D) banks; life
Answer: A. life; banks. Verse 14 reads “… they feared him, as they feared Moses, all the days of his life.”; verse 18 reads “… of the Jordan returned to their place, and went over all its banks, as before.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, twelve stones were taken from the middle of the ______ as a memorial. (4:9) Easy
Answer: Jordan. Joshua 4:9 records: “Joshua set up twelve stones in the midst of the Jordan, in the place where the feet of the priests who bore the ark of the covenant stood: and they are there to this day.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “For the ______ who bore the ark stood in the midst of the Jordan, until everything was finished that Yahweh commanded Joshua to speak to the …”? (4:10, WEB) Medium
A) shepherds B) ruler C) priests D) judge
Answer: C. priests. The passage specifies priests: “For the priests who bore the ark stood in the midst of the Jordan, until everything was finished that Yahweh commanded Joshua to speak to the …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… command you them, saying, Take hence out of the midst of the ______, out of the place where the priests' feet stood firm, twelve stones, and …” (4:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jordan. The cited words are “… command you them, saying, Take hence out of the midst of the Jordan, out of the place where the priests' feet stood firm, twelve stones, and …”
Joshua 5
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “This is the cause why ______ did circumcise: all the people who came forth out of Egypt, who were males, even all the men of war, …”? (5:4, WEB) Easy
A) Isaiah B) Joshua C) Asher D) Cyrus
Answer: B. Joshua. Joshua 5:4 uses Joshua: “This is the cause why Joshua did circumcise: all the people who came forth out of Egypt, who were males, even all the men of war, …”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… any more; but they ate of the fruit of the land of ______ that year.” (5:12); “… shoe from off your foot; for the place whereon you stand is ______. Joshua did so.” (5:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: Canaan; holy. The first passage reads “… any more; but they ate of the fruit of the land of Canaan that year.”; the second reads “… shoe from off your foot; for the place whereon you stand is holy. Joshua did so.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… Egypt from off you. Therefore the name of that place was called ______, to this day.” (5:9); “… land on the next day after the Passover, unleavened cakes and parched ______, in the same day.” (5:11, WEB) Hard
A) Gilgal; meat B) grain; Gilgal C) Damascus; grain D) Gilgal; grain
Answer: D. Gilgal; grain. Verse 9 reads “… Egypt from off you. Therefore the name of that place was called Gilgal, to this day.”; verse 11 reads “… land on the next day after the Passover, unleavened cakes and parched grain, in the same day.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Their children, whom he raised up in their place, them did Joshua prince: for they were uncircumcised, because they had not circumcised them by the way.” (5:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Their children, whom he raised up in their place, them did Joshua circumcise: for they were uncircumcised, because they had not circumcised them by the way.” It says circumcise, not prince.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… out were ______; but all the people who were born in the wilderness by the way as they came forth out of ______, they had not …” (5:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: circumcised; Egypt. The wording is “… out were circumcised; but all the people who were born in the wilderness by the way as they came forth out of Egypt, they had not …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… children of Israel walked forty years in the wilderness, until all the ______, even the men of war who came forth out of Egypt, were consumed, …”? (5:6, WEB) Medium
A) praise B) nation C) truth D) redemption
Answer: B. nation. The passage specifies nation: “… children of Israel walked forty years in the wilderness, until all the nation, even the men of war who came forth out of Egypt, were consumed, …”
Q7. True or false: Joshua met a man with a drawn sword who said he was commander of the LORD's army. (5:13-14) Easy
Answer: True. Joshua 5:13 records: “… Jericho, that he lifted up his eyes and looked, and, behold, there stood a man over against him with his sword drawn in his hand: and Joshua went to him, and said to him, Are you …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… of the Amorites, who were beyond the Jordan westward, and all the ______ of the Canaanites, who were by the sea, heard how that Yahweh had …” (5:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: kings. The cited words are “… of the Amorites, who were beyond the Jordan westward, and all the kings of the Canaanites, who were by the sea, heard how that Yahweh had …”
Joshua 6
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… horns before Yahweh passed on, and blew the trumpets: and the ______ of the ______ of Yahweh followed them.” (6:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: ark; covenant. The wording is “… horns before Yahweh passed on, and blew the trumpets: and the ark of the covenant of Yahweh followed them.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. True or false: Joshua's account of Jericho gives later readers an unrestricted command to destroy cities they dislike. (6:1-21) Hard
Answer: False. The account belongs to a particular ancient conquest narrative. The course discusses ancient battle rhetoric and the difficulty of the violence; it does not make this a general command for modern warfare.
Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the walls of ______ fell after the people marched around them. (6:20) Easy
Answer: Jericho. Joshua 6:20 records: “… trumpets; and it happened, when the people heard the sound of the trumpet, that the people shouted with a great shout, and the wall fell down flat, so that the people went up into the city, …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… the seventh day you shall compass the city seven times, and the ______ shall blow the trumpets.” (6:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: priests. The cited words are “… the seventh day you shall compass the city seven times, and the priests shall blow the trumpets.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… with the ram's horn, and when you hear the sound of the ______, all the people shall shout with a great shout; and the wall of …”? (6:5, WEB) Easy
A) morning B) father's C) edge D) trumpet
Answer: D. trumpet. Joshua 6:5 uses trumpet: “… with the ram's horn, and when you hear the sound of the trumpet, all the people shall shout with a great shout; and the wall of …”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The seventeen priests bearing the seven trumpets of rams' horns before the ark of Yahweh went on continually, and blew the trumpets: and the armed men …” (6:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “The seven priests bearing the seven trumpets of rams' horns before the ark of Yahweh went on continually, and blew the trumpets: and the armed men …” It says seven, not seventeen.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “______ commanded the people, saying, You shall not shout, nor let your voice be heard, neither shall any word proceed out of your mouth, until the …”? (6:10, WEB) Medium
A) Eve B) Nathan C) Joshua D) Hezekiah
Answer: C. Joshua. The passage specifies Joshua: “Joshua commanded the people, saying, You shall not shout, nor let your voice be heard, neither shall any word proceed out of your mouth, until the …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Joshua said to the ______ men who had spied out the land, Go into the prostitute's house, and bring out there the woman, and all that …” (6:22); “… burnt the city with fire, and all that was therein; only the ______, and the gold, and the vessels of brass and of iron, they put …” (6:24, WEB) Hard
Answer: two; silver. The first passage reads “Joshua said to the two men who had spied out the land, Go into the prostitute's house, and bring out there the woman, and all that …”; the second reads “… burnt the city with fire, and all that was therein; only the silver, and the gold, and the vessels of brass and of iron, they put …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Joshua 7
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “The men of Ai struck of them about thirty-______ men; and they chased them from before the gate even to Shebarim, and struck them at the …”? (7:5, WEB) Easy
A) eighty B) seventeen C) six D) fifty
Answer: C. six. Joshua 7:5 uses six: “The men of Ai struck of them about thirty-six men; and they chased them from before the gate even to Shebarim, and struck them at the …”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Joshua said to Achan, My son, please give ______ to Yahweh, the God of Israel, and make confession to him; and tell me now what you …” (7:19); “… all Israel with him, took Achan the son of Zerah, and the ______, and the mantle, and the wedge of gold, and his sons, and his …” (7:24, WEB) Hard
Answer: glory; silver. The first passage reads “Joshua said to Achan, My son, please give glory to Yahweh, the God of Israel, and make confession to him; and tell me now what you …”; the second reads “… all Israel with him, took Achan the son of Zerah, and the silver, and the mantle, and the wedge of gold, and his sons, and his …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… to Ai, which is beside Beth Aven, on the east side of ______, and spoke to them, saying, Go up and spy out the land. The …” (7:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Bethel. The cited words are “… to Ai, which is beside Beth Aven, on the east side of Bethel, and spoke to them, saying, Go up and spy out the land. The …”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… say, Sanctify yourselves against tomorrow: for thus says Yahweh, the God of Israel, There is a devoted thing in the midst of you, Israel; you can …” (7:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… say, Sanctify yourselves against tomorrow: for thus says Yahweh, the God of Israel, There is a devoted thing in the midst of you, Israel; you can …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Israel has sinned; yes, they have even transgressed my ______ which I commanded them: yes, they have even taken of the devoted thing, and have also …”? (7:11, WEB) Medium
A) judgment B) commandments C) testimonies D) covenant
Answer: D. covenant. The passage specifies covenant: “Israel has sinned; yes, they have even transgressed my covenant which I commanded them: yes, they have even taken of the devoted thing, and have also …”
Q6. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… he who is taken with the devoted thing shall be burnt with ______, he and all that he has; because he has transgressed the covenant of …” (7:15); “… the son of Zabdi, the son of Zerah, of the tribe of ______, was taken.” (7:18, WEB) Hard
A) fire; Judah B) fire; Job C) Judah; fire D) clothes; Judah
Answer: A. fire; Judah. Verse 15 reads “… he who is taken with the devoted thing shall be burnt with fire, he and all that he has; because he has transgressed the covenant of …”; verse 18 reads “… the son of Zabdi, the son of Zerah, of the tribe of Judah, was taken.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q7. True or false: Achan took devoted things from Jericho and hid them in his tent. (7:20-21) Easy
Answer: True. Joshua 7:20 records: “Achan answered Joshua, and said, Of a truth I have sinned against Yahweh, the God of Israel, and thus and thus have I done:”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “______ said, Alas, Lord Yahweh, why have you at all brought this people over the ______, to deliver us into the hand of the Amorites, to …” (7:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Joshua; Jordan. The wording is “Joshua said, Alas, Lord Yahweh, why have you at all brought this people over the Jordan, to deliver us into the hand of the Amorites, to …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Joshua 8
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Only the ______ and the spoil of that city Israel took for prey to themselves, according to the word of Yahweh which he commanded Joshua.” (8:27); “… any iron: and they offered thereon burnt offerings to Yahweh, and sacrificed peace-______.” (8:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: cattle; offerings. The first passage reads “Only the cattle and the spoil of that city Israel took for prey to themselves, according to the word of Yahweh which he commanded Joshua.”; the second reads “… any iron: and they offered thereon burnt offerings to Yahweh, and sacrificed peace-offerings.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… against Israel to battle, he and all his people, at the time ______, before the Arabah; but he didn't know that there was an ambush against …”? (8:14, WEB) Medium
A) appointed B) hurried C) fled D) turned
Answer: A. appointed. The passage specifies appointed: “… against Israel to battle, he and all his people, at the time appointed, before the Arabah; but he didn't know that there was an ambush against …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, joshua set an ambush for the city of ______. (8:2) Easy
Answer: Ai. Joshua 8:2 records: “… to Ai and her king as you did to Jericho and her king: only the spoil of it, and the cattle of it, shall you take for a prey to yourselves: set you an ambush for …”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… city; for they will say, They flee before us, as at the ______: so we will flee before them;”? (8:6, WEB) Easy
A) sixteen B) first C) third D) ninety
Answer: B. first. Joshua 8:6 uses first: “… city; for they will say, They flee before us, as at the first: so we will flee before them;”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “So ______ arose, and all the people of war, to go up to Ai: and Joshua chose out thirty thousand men, the mighty men of valor, …” (8:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Joshua. The cited words are “So Joshua arose, and all the people of war, to go up to Ai: and Joshua chose out thirty thousand men, the mighty men of valor, …”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “There was not a man left in Ai or Beth El, who didn't go out after Israel: and they left the city open, and pursued after …” (8:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “There was not a man left in Ai or Beth El, who didn't go out after Israel: and they left the city open, and pursued after …”
Q7. A) Persia B) Egypt C) Edom D) Moab
Answer: B. Egypt. Joshua 24:6 uses Egypt: “I brought your fathers out of Egypt: and you came to the sea; and the Egyptians pursued after your fathers with chariots and with horsemen to …”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Now therefore fear Yahweh, and serve him in sincerity and in kindness; and put away the gods which your fathers served beyond the River, and in …” (24:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Now therefore fear Yahweh, and serve him in sincerity and in truth; and put away the gods which your fathers served beyond the River, and in …” It says truth, not kindness.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “______ said to the people, You can't serve Yahweh; for he is a holy God; he is a jealous God; he will not forgive your disobedience …” (24:19); “… said he, the foreign gods which are among you, and incline your ______ to Yahweh, the God of Israel.” (24:23, WEB) Hard
A) heart; Joshua B) Saul; heart C) Joshua; heart D) Joshua; mouth
Answer: C. Joshua; heart. Verse 19 reads “Joshua said to the people, You can't serve Yahweh; for he is a holy God; he is a jealous God; he will not forgive your disobedience …”; verse 23 reads “… said he, the foreign gods which are among you, and incline your heart to Yahweh, the God of Israel.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Then ______ the son of Zippor, king of ______, arose and fought against Israel: and he sent and called Balaam the son of Beor to curse …” (24:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Balak; Moab. The wording is “Then Balak the son of Zippor, king of Moab, arose and fought against Israel: and he sent and called Balaam the son of Beor to curse …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “I gave you a ______ whereon you had not labored, and cities which you didn't build, and you dwell therein; of vineyards and olive groves which …”? (24:13, WEB) Medium
A) land B) earth C) mountain D) field
Answer: A. land. The passage specifies land: “I gave you a land whereon you had not labored, and cities which you didn't build, and you dwell therein; of vineyards and olive groves which …”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “I gave to Isaac Jacob and ______: and I gave to Esau Mount Seir, to possess it: and Jacob and his children went down into Egypt.” (24:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: Esau. The cited words are “I gave to Isaac Jacob and Esau: and I gave to Esau Mount Seir, to possess it: and Jacob and his children went down into Egypt.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Joshua wrote these words in the book of the ______ of God; and he took a great stone, and set it up there under the oak …” (24:26); “It happened after these things, that Joshua the son of Nun, the ______ of Yahweh, died, being one hundred ten years old.” (24:29, WEB) Hard

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q1. True or false: Judah and Simeon fought against the Canaanites in the land. (1:1-4) Easy

Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “It happened, when ______ had grown strong, that they put the Canaanites to forced labor, and did not utterly drive them out.” (1:28); “______ didn't drive out the inhabitants of Acco, nor the inhabitants of Sidon, nor of Ahlab, nor of Achzib, nor of Helbah, nor of Aphik, nor …” (1:31, WEB) Hard
Answer: Israel; Asher. The first passage reads “It happened, when Israel had grown strong, that they put the Canaanites to forced labor, and did not utterly drive them out.”; the second reads “Asher didn't drive out the inhabitants of Acco, nor the inhabitants of Sidon, nor of Ahlab, nor of Achzib, nor of Helbah, nor of Aphik, nor …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “But Adoni-bezek fled; and they ______ after him, and caught him, and cut off his thumbs and his great toes.” (1:6, WEB) Easy
Answer: pursued. The cited words are “But Adoni-bezek fled; and they pursued after him, and caught him, and cut off his thumbs and his great toes.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Adoni-bezek said, "Seventy ______, having their thumbs and their great toes cut off, gathered their food under my table: as I have done, so God has …”? (1:7, WEB) Easy
A) ruler B) kings C) king D) servants

Q5. Answer: D. Benjamin; pray. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Answer: True. The actual wording is “Judah went with Simeon his brother, and they struck the Canaanites who inhabited Zephath, and utterly destroyed it. The name of the city was called Hormah.”

Q3. Answer: altars. The angel quotes God's command and rebukes Israel for not obeying it. This is the specific action named in verse 2.

Q7. Judges 11
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Jephthah said to the elders of ______, Didn't you hate me, and drive me out of my father's house? and why are you come to me …”? (11:7, WEB) Easy

Q2. Multiple choice: What must be distinguished when interpreting Jephthah's vow? (11:29-40) Hard
A) Every vow is automatically morally right. B) The narrative records his vow and its tragic outcome without making it God's command. C) God explicitly commands Jephthah to make the vow. D) The chapter depicts a joyful, uncomplicated result.
Answer: B. The narrative records his vow and its tragic outcome without making it God's command.. Many readers understand the outcome as sacrifice; others argue for lifelong dedication. Both must reckon with the text's wording and grief. The Spirit's empowerment does not endorse every decision Jephthah makes.
Q3. True or false: Jephthah made a rash vow before going to battle. (11:30-31) Easy

Judges 15
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Samson said, With the jawbone of a donkey, heaps on ______, With the jawbone of a donkey I have struck a thousand men.” (15:16); “… thirsty, and called on Yahweh, and said, You have given this great ______ by the hand of your servant; and now shall I die for thirst, …” (15:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: heaps; deliverance. The first passage reads “Samson said, With the jawbone of a donkey, heaps on heaps, With the jawbone of a donkey I have struck a thousand men.”; the second reads “… thirsty, and called on Yahweh, and said, You have given this great deliverance by the hand of your servant; and now shall I die for thirst, …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Then three ninety men of Judah went down to the cleft of the rock of Etam, and said to Samson, "Don't you know that the Philistines …” (15:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Then three thousand men of Judah went down to the cleft of the rock of Etam, and said to Samson, "Don't you know that the Philistines …” It says thousand, not ninety.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Samson went and caught ______ hundred foxes, and took firebrands, and turned tail to tail, and put a firebrand in the midst between every two tails.”? (15:4, WEB) Easy
A) nineteen B) four C) ten D) three
Answer: D. three. Judges 15:4 uses three: “Samson went and caught three hundred foxes, and took firebrands, and turned tail to tail, and put a firebrand in the midst between every two tails.”
Q4. True or false: Samson killed a thousand men with the jawbone of a donkey. (15:15) Easy
Answer: True. Judges 15:15 records: “He found a fresh jawbone of a donkey, and put forth his hand, and took it, and struck a thousand men therewith.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Then the ______ said, Who has done this? They said, Samson, the son-in-______ of the Timnite, because he has taken his wife, and given her to …” (15:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: Philistines; law. The wording is “Then the Philistines said, Who has done this? They said, Samson, the son-in-law of the Timnite, because he has taken his wife, and given her to …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… therefore I gave her to your companion: isn't her younger sister more ______ than she? Please take her, instead.” (15:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: beautiful. The cited words are “… therefore I gave her to your companion: isn't her younger sister more beautiful than she? Please take her, instead.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… to him, We have come down to bind you, that we may ______ you into the hand of the Philistines. Samson said to them, Swear to …” (15:12); “… came to Lehi, the Philistines shouted as they met him: and the ______ of Yahweh came mightily on him, and the ropes that were on his …” (15:14, WEB) Hard
A) Spirit; deliver B) foxes; Spirit C) deliver; flax D) deliver; Spirit
Answer: D. deliver; Spirit. Verse 12 reads “… to him, We have come down to bind you, that we may deliver you into the hand of the Philistines. Samson said to them, Swear to …”; verse 14 reads “… came to Lehi, the Philistines shouted as they met him: and the Spirit of Yahweh came mightily on him, and the ropes that were on his …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “He struck them hip and ______ with a great slaughter: and he went down and lived in the cleft of the rock of Etam.”? (15:8, WEB) Medium
A) Judah B) burnt C) father D) thigh
Answer: D. thigh. The passage specifies thigh: “He struck them hip and thigh with a great slaughter: and he went down and lived in the cleft of the rock of Etam.”
Judges 16
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… god; for they said, Our god has delivered into our hand our ______, and the destroyer of our country, who has slain many of us.” (16:24); “… the Philistines were there; and there were on the roof about three ______ men and women, who saw while Samson made sport.” (16:27, WEB) Hard
Answer: enemy; thousand. The first passage reads “… god; for they said, Our god has delivered into our hand our enemy, and the destroyer of our country, who has slain many of us.”; the second reads “… the Philistines were there; and there were on the roof about three thousand men and women, who saw while Samson made sport.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “She said to him, How can you say, I glory you, when your heart is not with me? you have mocked me these three times, and …” (16:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “She said to him, How can you say, I love you, when your heart is not with me? you have mocked me these three times, and …” It says love, not glory.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “When Delilah saw that he had told her all his ______, she sent and called for the lords of the Philistines, saying, Come up this once, …” (16:18); “… they brought him down to Gaza, and bound him with fetters of ______; and he did grind in the prison-house.” (16:21, WEB) Hard
A) heart; brass B) brass; heart C) heart; wood D) blood; brass
Answer: A. heart; brass. Verse 18 reads “When Delilah saw that he had told her all his heart, she sent and called for the lords of the Philistines, saying, Come up this once, …”; verse 21 reads “… they brought him down to Gaza, and bound him with fetters of brass; and he did grind in the prison-house.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… city, and were quiet all the night, saying, Let be until morning ______, then we will kill him.” (16:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: light. The cited words are “… city, and were quiet all the night, saying, Let be until morning light, then we will kill him.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… and we will each give you of us eleven hundred pieces of ______.”? (16:5, WEB) Easy
A) sand B) gold C) silver D) copper
Answer: C. silver. Judges 16:5 uses silver: “… and we will each give you of us eleven hundred pieces of silver.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, delilah discovered the secret of Samson's ______. (16:6) Easy
Answer: strength. Judges 16:6 records: “Delilah said to Samson, Tell me, Please, in which your great strength lies, and with which you might be bound to afflict you.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… in the inner chamber. He broke them off his arms like a ______.”? (16:12, WEB) Medium
A) carried B) work C) thread D) Manoah
Answer: C. thread. The passage specifies thread: “… in the inner chamber. He broke them off his arms like a thread.”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… him, The ______ are on you, Samson. He broke the cords, as a string of tow is broken when it touches the ______. So his strength …” (16:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Philistines; fire. The wording is “… him, The Philistines are on you, Samson. He broke the cords, as a string of tow is broken when it touches the fire. So his strength …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Judges 17
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… a curse, and did also speak it in my ears, behold, the ______ is with me; I took it. His mother said, Blessed be my son …” (17:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: silver. The cited words are “… a curse, and did also speak it in my ears, behold, the silver is with me; I took it. His mother said, Blessed be my son …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and he came to the hill-country of Ephraim to the house of ______, as he traveled.”? (17:8, WEB) Medium
A) Miriam B) Micah C) Simeon D) Manasseh
Answer: B. Micah. The passage specifies Micah: “… and he came to the hill-country of Ephraim to the house of Micah, as he traveled.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “In those days there was no king in Israel: every man did that which was right in his own eyes.” (17:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “In those days there was no king in Israel: every man did that which was right in his own eyes.”
Q4. True or false: Micah kept an idol in his house. (17:4-5) Easy
Answer: True. Judges 17:4 records: “… restored the money to his mother, his mother took two hundred pieces of silver, and gave them to the founder, who made of it an engraved image and a molten image: and it was in the …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… him, Dwell with me, and be to me a father and a ______, and I will give you ten pieces of silver by the year, and …” (17:10); “… to him, Whence come you? He said to him, I am a ______ of Bethlehem Judah, and I go to sojourn where I may find a …” (17:9, WEB) Hard
A) priest; Judah B) priests; Levite C) priest; Levite D) Levite; priest
Answer: C. priest; Levite. Verse 10 reads “… him, Dwell with me, and be to me a father and a priest, and I will give you ten pieces of silver by the year, and …”; verse 9 reads “… to him, Whence come you? He said to him, I am a Levite of Bethlehem Judah, and I go to sojourn where I may find a …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… man out of Bethlehem Judah, of the family of ______, who was a Levite; and he ______ there.” (17:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Judah; sojourned. The wording is “… man out of Bethlehem Judah, of the family of Judah, who was a Levite; and he sojourned there.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Micah ______ the Levite, and the young man became his priest, and was in the house of Micah.” (17:12); “Then said Micah, Now know I that Yahweh will do me good, ______ I have a Levite to my priest.” (17:13, WEB) Hard
Answer: consecrated; seeing. The first passage reads “Micah consecrated the Levite, and the young man became his priest, and was in the house of Micah.”; the second reads “Then said Micah, Now know I that Yahweh will do me good, seeing I have a Levite to my priest.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He restored the eleven ______ pieces of silver to his mother; and his mother said, I most assuredly dedicate the silver to Yahweh from my hand …”? (17:3, WEB) Easy
A) fourth B) hundred C) sixty D) fourteen
Answer: B. hundred. Judges 17:3 uses hundred: “He restored the eleven hundred pieces of silver to his mother; and his mother said, I most assuredly dedicate the silver to Yahweh from my hand …”
Judges 18
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… engraved image, the ephod, and the teraphim, and the molten image, the ______ said to them, What do you?” (18:18); “The priest's ______ was glad, and he took the ephod, and the teraphim, and the engraved image, and went in the midst of the people.” (18:20, WEB) Hard
A) priest; back B) judge; heart C) priest; heart D) heart; priest
Answer: C. priest; heart. Verse 18 reads “… engraved image, the ephod, and the teraphim, and the molten image, the priest said to them, What do you?”; verse 20 reads “The priest's heart was glad, and he took the ephod, and the teraphim, and the engraved image, and went in the midst of the people.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… good: and are you still? don't be ______ to go and to enter in to possess the ______.” (18:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: slothful; land. The wording is “… good: and are you still? don't be slothful to go and to enter in to possess the land.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and encamped in Kiriath Jearim, in Judah: therefore they called that place Mahaneh-______, to this day; behold, it is behind Kiriath Jearim.”? (18:12, WEB) Medium
A) Sidonians B) Kiriath C) hand D) dan
Answer: D. dan. The passage specifies dan: “… and encamped in Kiriath Jearim, in Judah: therefore they called that place Mahaneh-dan, to this day; behold, it is behind Kiriath Jearim.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… among us, lest angry fellows fall on you, and you lose your ______, with the lives of your household."” (18:25); “… no dealings with any man; and it was in the valley that ______ by Beth Rehob. They built the city, and lived therein.” (18:28, WEB) Hard
Answer: life; lies. The first passage reads “… among us, lest angry fellows fall on you, and you lose your life, with the lives of your household."”; the second reads “… no dealings with any man; and it was in the valley that lies by Beth Rehob. They built the city, and lived therein.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Then the ______ men departed, and came to Laish, and saw the people who were therein, how they lived in security, after the manner of the …”? (18:7, WEB) Easy
A) twelfth B) seven C) five D) eighteen
Answer: C. five. Judges 18:7 uses five: “Then the five men departed, and came to Laish, and saw the people who were therein, how they lived in security, after the manner of the …”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… that there is in these houses an ephod, and teraphim, and an engraved image, and a molten image? now therefore consider what you have to do.” (18:14, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… that there is in these houses an ephod, and teraphim, and an engraved image, and a molten image? now therefore consider what you have to do.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “When they were by the house of ______, they knew the voice of the young man the Levite; and they turned aside there, and said to …” (18:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Micah. The cited words are “When they were by the house of Micah, they knew the voice of the young man the Levite; and they turned aside there, and said to …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the Danites captured the city of ______ and made it their home. (18:27) Easy
Answer: Laish. Judges 18:27 records: “… took that which Micah had made, and the priest whom he had, and came to Laish, to a people quiet and secure, and struck them with the edge of the sword; and they burnt the city …”
Judges 19
Q1. Multiple choice: How should the abuse in Gibeah be read within Judges' closing account? (19:22-30; 21:25) Hard
A) As an example of proper hospitality. B) As a command to repeat the men's actions. C) As evidence of moral collapse, not a model of faithful conduct. D) As proof that every Israelite acts faithfully.
Answer: C. As evidence of moral collapse, not a model of faithful conduct.. The narrative exposes brutal failures toward a vulnerable woman. Recording these actions does not endorse them; the book's closing refrain highlights the community's disorder.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and let us turn aside into this city of the Jebusites, and ______ in it.”? (19:11, WEB) Medium
A) abused B) departed C) lodge D) won't
Answer: C. lodge. The passage specifies lodge: “… and let us turn aside into this city of the Jebusites, and lodge in it.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… hill-country of Ephraim; from there am I, and I went to Bethlehem ______: and I am now going to the house of Yahweh; and there is …” (19:18); “The old man said, ______ be to you; howsoever let all your wants lie on me; only don't lodge in the street.” (19:20, WEB) Hard
A) Judah; grief B) Peace; Judah C) Judah; Peace D) Hezekiah; Peace
Answer: C. Judah; Peace. Verse 18 reads “… hill-country of Ephraim; from there am I, and I went to Bethlehem Judah: and I am now going to the house of Yahweh; and there is …”; verse 20 reads “The old man said, Peace be to you; howsoever let all your wants lie on me; only don't lodge in the street.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… man rose up to depart, he, and his concubine, and his ______, his father-in-______, the young lady's father, said to him, Behold, now the day draws …” (19:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: servant; law. The wording is “… man rose up to depart, he, and his concubine, and his servant, his father-in-law, the young lady's father, said to him, Behold, now the day draws …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “They turned aside there, to go in to lodge in consider: and he went in, and sat him down in the street of the city; for …” (19:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “They turned aside there, to go in to lodge in Gibeah: and he went in, and sat him down in the street of the city; for …” It says Gibeah, not consider.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… father said to his son-in-law, Strengthen your heart with a morsel of ______, and afterward you shall go your way.”? (19:5, WEB) Easy
A) olives B) bread C) corn D) salt
Answer: B. bread. Judges 19:5 uses bread: “… father said to his son-in-law, Strengthen your heart with a morsel of bread, and afterward you shall go your way.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… her, to speak kindly to her, to bring her again, having his ______ with him, and a couple of donkeys: and she brought him into her …” (19:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: servant. The cited words are “… her, to speak kindly to her, to bring her again, having his servant with him, and a couple of donkeys: and she brought him into her …”
Q8. True or false: A Levite's concubine was abused in Gibeah. (19:22-25) Easy
Answer: True. Judges 19:22 records: “… the men of the city, certain base fellows, beset the house round about, beating at the door; and they spoke to the master of the house, the old man, saying, Bring forth the man who came …”
Judges 20
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The children of Israel arose, and went up to ______, and asked counsel of God; and they said, Who shall go up for us first to …”? (20:18, WEB) Medium
A) Zion B) Damascus C) Bethel D) Edom
Answer: C. Bethel. The passage specifies Bethel: “The children of Israel arose, and went up to Bethel, and asked counsel of God; and they said, Who shall go up for us first to …”
Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the tribes of Israel gathered against the tribe of ______. (20:11) Easy
Answer: Benjamin. Judges 20:11 records: “So all the men of Israel were gathered against the city, knit together as one man.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “(Now the children of ______ heard that the children of Israel had gone up to Mizpah.) The children of Israel said, Tell us, how was this …” (20:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Benjamin. The cited words are “(Now the children of Benjamin heard that the children of Israel had gone up to Mizpah.) The children of Israel said, Tell us, how was this …”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “The ______-in-wait hurried, and rushed on Gibeah; and the liers-in-wait drew themselves along, and struck all the city with the edge of the sword.” (20:37); “… of Israel to the way of the wilderness; but the battle followed ______ after them; and those who came out of the cities destroyed them in …” (20:42, WEB) Hard
Answer: liers; hard. The first passage reads “The liers-in-wait hurried, and rushed on Gibeah; and the liers-in-wait drew themselves along, and struck all the city with the edge of the sword.”; the second reads “… of Israel to the way of the wilderness; but the battle followed hard after them; and those who came out of the cities destroyed them in …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The children of Israel went up and wept before Yahweh until even; and they asked of Yahweh, saying, Shall I again draw near to battle against …” (20:23, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “The children of Israel went up and wept before Yahweh until even; and they asked of Yahweh, saying, Shall I again draw near to battle against …”
Q6. Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and we will take ______ men of one hundred throughout all the tribes of Israel, and one hundred of one thousand, and a thousand out of …”? (20:10, WEB) Easy

Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Now therefore ______ up the men, the base fellows, who are in Gibeah, that we may put them to ______, and put away evil from Israel. …” (20:13, WEB) Medium

Judges 21
Q1. A) Jeremiah; inheritance B) Benjamin; redemption C) Benjamin; inheritance D) inheritance; Benjamin

These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… up to Yahweh to Mizpah, saying, He shall surely be put to ______.”? (21:5, WEB) Easy

Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… is the thing that you shall do: you shall utterly destroy every highway, and every woman who has lain by man.” (21:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… is the thing that you shall do: you shall utterly destroy every male, and every woman who has lain by man.” It says male, not highway.
Q7. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Ruth
Ruth 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Yahweh grant you that you may find ______, each of you in the house of her husband. Then she kissed them, and they lifted up their …”? (1:9, WEB) Medium
A) curse B) power C) worship D) rest
Answer: D. rest. The passage specifies rest: “Yahweh grant you that you may find rest, each of you in the house of her husband. Then she kissed them, and they lifted up their …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “would you therefore wait until they were ______? would you therefore stay from having husbands? nay, my daughters, for it grieves me much for your sakes, …” (1:13); “______ said, "Don't entreat me to leave you, and to return from following after you, for where you go, I will go; and where you lodge, …” (1:16, WEB) Hard
A) Ruth; grown B) died; Ruth C) grown; Ruth D) grown; Balak
Answer: C. grown; Ruth. Verse 13 reads “would you therefore wait until they were grown? would you therefore stay from having husbands? nay, my daughters, for it grieves me much for your sakes, …”; verse 16 reads “Ruth said, "Don't entreat me to leave you, and to return from following after you, for where you go, I will go; and where you lodge, …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Naomi said, Turn again, my daughters: why will you go with me? have I yet sons in my womb, that they may be your husbands?” (1:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Naomi said, Turn again, my daughters: why will you go with me? have I yet sons in my womb, that they may be your husbands?”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “They took them wives of the women of ______; the name of the one was Orpah, and the name of the other Ruth: and they lived …”? (1:4, WEB) Easy
A) Moab B) Goshen C) Edom D) Canaan
Answer: A. Moab. Ruth 1:4 uses Moab: “They took them wives of the women of Moab; the name of the one was Orpah, and the name of the other Ruth: and they lived …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Then she arose with her daughters-in-______, that she might return from the country of ______: for she had heard in the country of Moab how that …” (1:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: law; Moab. The wording is “Then she arose with her daughters-in-law, that she might return from the country of Moab: for she had heard in the country of Moab how that …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. True or false: Naomi told her daughters-in-law to return to their homes. (1:8) Easy

Q4. Multiple choice: How does the ending connect an ordinary family redemption with Israel's wider story? (4:13-22) Hard
A) Ruth and Boaz become ancestors of David. B) Ruth is removed from the genealogy because she is Moabite. C) Boaz becomes the first king of Egypt. D) The family is cut off without descendants.
Answer: A. Ruth and Boaz become ancestors of David.. The genealogy links this family's restoration to the royal line. Matthew 1 later includes Ruth in Jesus' genealogy, a Christian connection that follows the story's original family setting.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… Elimelech's, and all that was Chilion's and Mahlon's, of the hand of ______.”? (4:9, WEB) Medium

Q4. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… brought about the ark of the God of Israel to us, to ______ us and our people.” (5:10); “… deadly confusion throughout all the city; the hand of God was very ______ there.” (5:11, WEB) Hard

Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… behold, Dagon was fallen on his face to the ground before the ______ of Yahweh. They took Dagon, and set him in his place again.” (5:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: ark. The cited words are “… behold, Dagon was fallen on his face to the ground before the ark of Yahweh. They took Dagon, and set him in his place again.”
Q6. True or false: The Philistines placed the ark in the temple of Dagon. (5:2) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Samuel 5:2 records: “The Philistines took the ark of God, and brought it into the house of Dagon, and set it by Dagon.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “They sent therefore and gathered all the lords of the ______ to them, and said, What shall we do with the ark of the God of …” (5:8); “… struck the men of the city, both small and great; and tumors ______ out on them.” (5:9, WEB) Hard
A) broke; Philistines B) Philistines; Ebenezer C) tumors; broke D) Philistines; broke
Answer: D. Philistines; broke. Verse 8 reads “They sent therefore and gathered all the lords of the Philistines to them, and said, What shall we do with the ark of the God of …”; verse 9 reads “… struck the men of the city, both small and great; and tumors broke out on them.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “When they arose ______ on the next day morning, behold, Dagon was fallen on his face to the ground before the ark of Yahweh; and the …”? (5:4, WEB) Easy
A) Dagon B) gathered C) Ebenezer D) early
Answer: D. early. 1 Samuel 5:4 uses early: “When they arose early on the next day morning, behold, Dagon was fallen on his face to the ground before the ark of Yahweh; and the …”

1 Samuel 8
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Then all the elders of Israel gathered themselves together, and came to ______ to Ramah;”? (8:4, WEB) Easy
A) Samuel B) Adam C) Aaron D) Laban
Answer: A. Samuel. 1 Samuel 8:4 uses Samuel: “Then all the elders of Israel gathered themselves together, and came to Samuel to Ramah;”
Q2. Multiple choice: How can Israel's request for a king be described as a rejection of God even though kingship later has a place in God's purposes? (8:4-9, 19-22) Hard
A) God cannot work through any human ruler. B) The people demand a king like the nations and reject God's kingship in this request. C) Samuel had never served Israel. D) The people ask for deeper obedience to every warning.
Answer: B. The people demand a king like the nations and reject God's kingship in this request.. The judgment concerns the motives and posture expressed in this episode. It does not mean that every later royal institution is automatically outside God's plan.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Now the name of his firstborn was ______; and the name of his second, Abijah: they were judges in Beersheba.” (8:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Joel. The cited words are “Now the name of his firstborn was Joel; and the name of his second, Abijah: they were judges in Beersheba.”

Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… to their voice: however you shall protest solemnly to them, and shall ______ them the manner of the king who shall reign over them.”? (8:9, WEB) Medium
A) Joel B) show C) fifties D) captains

Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the people asked for a ______ to rule over them. (8:5) Easy
Answer: king. 1 Samuel 8:5 records: “and they said to him, Behold, you are old, and your sons don't walk in your ways: now make us a king to judge us like all the nations.”

The first passage reads “… and there was no strength in him; for he had eaten no bread all the day, nor all the night.”; the second reads “… also to the voice of your handmaid, and let me set a morsel of bread before you; and eat, that you may have strength, when you …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

A) blood; walls B) hair; book C) book; blood D) blood; book
Answer: D. blood; book. Verse 16 reads “David said to him, Your blood be on your head; for your mouth has testified against you, saying, I have slain Yahweh's anointed.”; verse 18 reads “… Judah the song of the bow: behold, it is written in the book of Jashar):”. Answer: beside; life. The wording is “… me, Stand, I pray you, beside me, and kill me; for anguish has taken hold of me, because my life is yet whole in me.” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: False. The actual wording is “David said to him, How were you not afraid to put forth your hand to destroy Yahweh's anointed?” It says David, not Eli.

Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “David perceived that Yahweh had established him king over Jesse, and that he had exalted his kingdom for his people Israel's sake.” (5:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “David perceived that Yahweh had established him king over Israel, and that he had exalted his kingdom for his people Israel's sake.” It says Israel, not Jesse.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “In ______ he reigned over Judah seven years and six months; and in Jerusalem he reigned thirty-three years over all Israel and Judah.”? (5:5, WEB) Easy
A) Goshen B) Persia C) Hebron D) Edom
Answer: C. Hebron. 2 Samuel 5:5 uses Hebron: “In Hebron he reigned over Judah seven years and six months; and in Jerusalem he reigned thirty-three years over all Israel and Judah.”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… breach of waters. Therefore he called the name of that place Baal ______.” (5:20); “… a circuit behind them, and come on them over against the mulberry ______.” (5:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: Perazim; trees. The first passage reads “… breach of waters. Therefore he called the name of that place Baal Perazim.”; the second reads “… a circuit behind them, and come on them over against the mulberry trees.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the watercourse, and ______ the lame and the blind, who are hated of David's soul. Therefore they say, There are the ______ and the lame; he …” (5:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: strike; blind. The wording is “… the watercourse, and strike the lame and the blind, who are hated of David's soul. Therefore they say, There are the blind and the lame; he …” The missing terms belong in that order.

2 Samuel 7

Q2. True or false: God made a covenant with David about a house that would last forever. (7:12-16) Easy
Answer: True. 2 Samuel 7:12 records: “When your days are fulfilled, and you shall sleep with your fathers, I will set up your seed after you, who shall proceed out of your bowels, and I will establish his kingdom.”
Q3. Multiple choice: What does the repeated word house mean in David's plan and God's promise? (7:1-16) Hard
A) David proposes a temple, while God promises David a dynasty. B) Both uses mean only a private bedroom. C) The word always means an army. D) God promises that David will build every temple.
Answer: A. David proposes a temple, while God promises David a dynasty.. The wordplay shifts attention from what David will build to what God promises to establish. Christians connect the enduring royal promise with Jesus; the initial setting concerns David's family and kingdom.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the day that I brought up the children of Israel out of ______, even to this day, but have walked in a tent and in a …”? (7:6, WEB) Easy

Answer: Nathan. The cited words are “that the king said to Nathan the prophet, See now, I dwell in a house of cedar, but the ark of God dwells within curtains.”

2 Samuel 12
Q1. Multiple choice: How do Nathan's announcement of forgiveness and his warning about consequences fit together? (12:7-14) Hard
A) Forgiveness means Nathan's warnings are withdrawn. B) David is forgiven, yet the damage and announced judgment are not erased. C) David is told he has committed no wrong. D) Consequences prove that no forgiveness is offered.
Answer: B. David is forgiven, yet the damage and announced judgment are not erased.. The passage states both forgiveness and consequences. The course uses it to show why repentance does not make the effects of wrongdoing unreal.
Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, nathan said to David, You are the ______. (12:7) Easy

Verse 23 reads “It happened after two full years, that Absalom had sheep-shearers in Baal Hazor, which is beside Ephraim: and Absalom invited all the king's sons.”; verse 28 reads “… his servants, saying, Mark you now, when Amnon's heart is merry with wine; and when I tell you, Smite Amnon, then kill him; don't be afraid; …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

Q3. Multiple choice: How does Solomon distinguish the temple from a building that could contain God? (8:27-30) Hard
A) He says the temple created God. B) He asks Israel to stop praying. C) He says God cannot hear outside Jerusalem. D) He says even the heavens cannot contain God and asks God to hear prayer.
Answer: D. He says even the heavens cannot contain God and asks God to hear prayer.. The temple is a chosen focus of worship, not a container that limits God's being. Solomon's prayer holds God's transcendence and attention to His people together.

Q2. Multiple choice: How do Solomon's later choices relate to the kingly restrictions in Deuteronomy 17? (11:1-10; Deuteronomy 17:16-17) Hard

Answer: A. His many wives and turned heart contradict the warning given to kings.. The narrative explicitly connects his wives with his heart turning to other gods. The course evaluates his apparent success by covenant faithfulness rather than wealth alone.

A) ate; eaten B) eaten; ate C) ate; king's D) works; eaten

Verse 19 reads “So he went back with him, and ate bread in his house, and drank water.”; verse 22 reads “but came back, and have eaten bread and drunk water in the place of which he said to you, Eat no bread, and drink no water; …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.

The first passage reads “As the king of Israel was passing by on the wall, there cried a woman to him, saying, Help, my lord, O king.”; the second reads “So we boiled my son, and ate him: and I said to her on the next day, Give your son, that we may eat him; and …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… a day of good news, and we hold our ______: if we wait until the morning ______, punishment will overtake us; now therefore come, let us …” (7:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: peace; light. The wording is “… a day of good news, and we hold our peace: if we wait until the morning light, punishment will overtake us; now therefore come, let us …” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: C. horses. The passage specifies horses: “… behold, there was no man there, neither voice of man, but the horses tied, and the donkeys tied, and the tents as they were.”

Answer: False. The actual wording is “The king arose in the night, and said to his servants, I will now show you what the Syrians have done to us. They know that …” It says servants, not soldier.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “One of his servants answered, Please let some take ______ of the horses that remain, which are left in the city (behold, they are as all …” (7:13); “They took therefore two chariots with horses; and the ______ sent after the host of the Syrians, saying, Go and see.” (7:14, WEB) Hard

Answer: Samaria; Israel. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

A) judgment B) statutes C) promises D) promise

Q2. True or false: After reporting Assyria's capture of Samaria, the narrator describes Israel's idolatry and covenant unfaithfulness. (17:6-23) Easy
Answer: True. Verse 6 reports exile, while verses 7-23 give the theological explanation. The cause should not be attributed to verse 6 alone.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… Yahweh their God, who brought them up out of the land of ______ from under the hand of Pharaoh king of Egypt, and had feared other …”? (17:7, WEB) Easy

Answer: True. The actual wording is “Also Judah didn't keep the commandments of Yahweh their God, but walked in the statutes of Israel which they made.”
Q6. Multiple choice: What explanation does the narrator give for the northern kingdom's exile? (17:6-23) Hard
A) Israel had kept every command perfectly. B) Persistent covenant unfaithfulness despite God's warnings through prophets. C) The prophets had told Israel to worship idols. D) The narrator says there is no reason.
Answer: B. Persistent covenant unfaithfulness despite God's warnings through prophets.. Verse 6 reports the deportation; the following verses interpret it in terms of idolatry and refused warnings. Political defeat and the book's theological explanation should be distinguished.

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
