Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the Book of the Law was found in the house of the ______. (22:8) Easy
Answer: LORD. 2 Kings 22:8 records: “Hilkiah the high priest said to Shaphan the scribe, I have found the book of the law in the house of Yahweh. Hilkiah delivered the book to Shaphan, and he read it.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the son of Micaiah, and Shaphan the scribe, and Asaiah the king's ______, saying,” (22:12); “… Harhas, keeper of the wardrobe (now she lived in Jerusalem in the ______ quarter); and they talked with her.” (22:14, WEB) Hard
A) fathers; second B) second; servant C) servant; second D) servant; seventh
Answer: C. servant; second. Verse 12 reads “… the son of Micaiah, and Shaphan the scribe, and Asaiah the king's servant, saying,”; verse 14 reads “… Harhas, keeper of the wardrobe (now she lived in Jerusalem in the second quarter); and they talked with her.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… in the eyes of Yahweh, and walked in all the way of ______ his father, and didn't turn aside to the right hand or to the …” (22:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: David. The cited words are “… in the eyes of Yahweh, and walked in all the way of David his father, and didn't turn aside to the right hand or to the …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… it, even all the words of the book which the king of ______ has read.” (22:16); “because your ______ was tender, and you did humble yourself before Yahweh, when you heard what I spoke against this place, and against the inhabitants of …” (22:19, WEB) Hard
Answer: Judah; heart. The first passage reads “… it, even all the words of the book which the king of Judah has read.”; the second reads “because your heart was tender, and you did humble yourself before Yahweh, when you heard what I spoke against this place, and against the inhabitants of …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Go up to Hilkiah the high ______, that he may sum the money which is brought into the house of Yahweh, which the keepers of the …”? (22:4, WEB) Easy
A) priest B) prince C) sisters D) shepherds
Answer: A. priest. 2 Kings 22:4 uses priest: “Go up to Hilkiah the high priest, that he may sum the money which is brought into the house of Yahweh, which the keepers of the …”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Shaphan the scribe told the king, saying, Hilkiah the priest has delivered me a book. Shaphan read it before the king.” (22:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Shaphan the scribe told the king, saying, Hilkiah the priest has delivered me a book. Shaphan read it before the king.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… to the king, and brought the king word again, and said, Your ______ have emptied out the money that was found in the house, and have …”? (22:9, WEB) Medium
A) servants B) elders C) sisters D) king
Answer: A. servants. The passage specifies servants: “… to the king, and brought the king word again, and said, Your servants have emptied out the money that was found in the house, and have …”
2 Kings 23
Q1. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… up against the king of Assyria to the river Euphrates: and king ______ went against him; and Pharaoh Necoh killed him at Megiddo, when he had …” (23:29); “… and put the land to a tribute of one hundred talents of ______, and a talent of gold.” (23:33, WEB) Hard
Answer: Josiah; silver. The first passage reads “… up against the king of Assyria to the river Euphrates: and king Josiah went against him; and Pharaoh Necoh killed him at Megiddo, when he had …”; the second reads “… and put the land to a tribute of one hundred talents of silver, and a talent of gold.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “He brought out the Asherah from the house of Yahweh, outside of ______, to the brook Kidron, and burned it at the brook Kidron, and beat …”? (23:6, WEB) Easy
A) Damascus B) Jerusalem C) Moab D) Bethel
Answer: B. Jerusalem. 2 Kings 23:6 uses Jerusalem: “He brought out the Asherah from the house of Yahweh, outside of Jerusalem, to the brook Kidron, and burned it at the brook Kidron, and beat …”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “He broke in ______ the pillars, and cut down the Asherim, and filled their places with the bones of men.”? (23:14, WEB) Medium
A) graves B) pieces C) idolatrous D) inhabitants
Answer: B. pieces. The passage specifies pieces: “He broke in pieces the pillars, and cut down the Asherim, and filled their places with the bones of men.”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… bones alone, with the bones of the prophet who came out of Samaria.” (23:18, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… bones alone, with the bones of the prophet who came out of Samaria.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the days of the kings of Israel, nor of the kings of ______;” (23:22); “… all his soul, and with all his might, according to all the ______ of Moses; neither after him arose there any like him.” (23:25, WEB) Hard
A) Abraham; law B) Judah; law C) law; Judah D) Judah; commandment
Answer: B. Judah; law. Verse 22 reads “… the days of the kings of Israel, nor of the kings of Judah;”; verse 25 reads “… all his soul, and with all his might, according to all the law of Moses; neither after him arose there any like him.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… that the ______ of Judah had given to the sun, at the entrance of the house of Yahweh, by the chamber of ______-melech the chamberlain, which …” (23:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: kings; Nathan. The wording is “… that the kings of Judah had given to the sun, at the entrance of the house of Yahweh, by the chamber of Nathan-melech the chamberlain, which …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. True or false: Josiah read the Book of the Law to all the people. (23:2) Easy
Answer: True. 2 Kings 23:2 records: “… men of Judah and all the inhabitants of Jerusalem with him, and the priests, and the prophets, and all the people, both small and great: and he read in their ears all the words of the …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… were written in this book: and all the people stood to the ______.” (23:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: covenant. The cited words are “… were written in this book: and all the people stood to the covenant.”
2 Kings 24
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… it, according to the word of Yahweh, which he spoke by his ______ the prophets.” (24:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: servants. The cited words are “… it, according to the word of Yahweh, which he spoke by his servants the prophets.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Jehoiachin was ______ years old when he began to reign; and he reigned in Jerusalem three months: and his mother's name was Nehushta the daughter of …”? (24:8, WEB) Medium
A) eighteen B) ninety C) twelfth D) ten
Answer: A. eighteen. The passage specifies eighteen: “Jehoiachin was eighteen years old when he began to reign; and he reigned in Jerusalem three months: and his mother's name was Nehushta the daughter of …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, jehoiachin was taken captive to ______. (24:15) Easy
Answer: Babylon. 2 Kings 24:15 records: “He carried away Jehoiachin to Babylon; and the king's mother, and the king's wives, and his officers, and the chief men of the land, carried he into captivity from Jerusalem to Babylon.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… the innocent blood that he shed; for he filled Jerusalem with innocent ______: and Yahweh would not pardon.”? (24:4, WEB) Easy
A) neck B) head C) hands D) blood
Answer: D. blood. 2 Kings 24:4 uses blood: “… the innocent blood that he shed; for he filled Jerusalem with innocent blood: and Yahweh would not pardon.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The king of ______ didn't come again out of his land any more; for the king of ______ had taken, from the brook of Egypt to …” (24:7, WEB) Medium
Answer: Egypt; Babylon. The wording is “The king of Egypt didn't come again out of his land any more; for the king of Babylon had taken, from the brook of Egypt to …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “At that time the servants of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon came up to Jerusalem, and the city was besieged.” (24:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “At that time the servants of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon came up to Jerusalem, and the city was besieged.”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… and his officers: and the king of Babylon took him in the ______ year of his reign.” (24:12); “… and all the princes, and all the mighty men of valor, even ______ thousand captives, and all the craftsmen and the smiths; none remained, save the …” (24:14, WEB) Hard
A) eighth; ten B) ten; eighth C) four; ten D) eighth; three
Answer: A. eighth; ten. Verse 12 reads “… and his officers: and the king of Babylon took him in the eighth year of his reign.”; verse 14 reads “… and all the princes, and all the mighty men of valor, even ten thousand captives, and all the craftsmen and the smiths; none remained, save the …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “All the men of might, even ______ thousand, and the craftsmen and the smiths one thousand, all of them strong and apt for war, even them …” (24:16); “… was twenty-one years old when he began to reign; and he reigned ______ years in Jerusalem: and his mother's name was Hamutal the daughter of Jeremiah …” (24:18, WEB) Hard
Answer: seven; eleven. The first passage reads “All the men of might, even seven thousand, and the craftsmen and the smiths one thousand, all of them strong and apt for war, even them …”; the second reads “… was twenty-one years old when he began to reign; and he reigned eleven years in Jerusalem: and his mother's name was Hamutal the daughter of Jeremiah …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
2 Kings 25
Q1. True or false: Jerusalem's walls were broken down by the Babylonians. (25:10) Easy
Answer: True. 2 Kings 25:10 records: “All the army of the Chaldeans, who were with the captain of the guard, broke down the walls of Jerusalem round about.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… Chaldeans pursued after the king, and overtook him in the plains of ______; and all his army was scattered from him.”? (25:5, WEB) Easy
A) Damascus B) Jericho C) Assyria D) Hebron
Answer: B. Jericho. 2 Kings 25:5 uses Jericho: “… Chaldeans pursued after the king, and overtook him in the plains of Jericho; and all his army was scattered from him.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… that which was of gold, in gold, and that which was of silver, in silver, the captain of the guard took away.” (25:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… that which was of gold, in gold, and that which was of silver, in silver, the captain of the guard took away.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… in the city, and those who fell away, who fell to the ______ of Babylon, and the residue of the multitude, did Nebuzaradan the captain of …”? (25:11, WEB) Medium
A) king B) daughters C) prophet D) brother
Answer: A. king. The passage specifies king: “… in the city, and those who fell away, who fell to the king of Babylon, and the residue of the multitude, did Nebuzaradan the captain of …”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… day of the month, which was the nineteenth year of king ______, king of ______, came Nebuzaradan the captain of the guard, a servant of the …” (25:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: Nebuchadnezzar; Babylon. The wording is “… day of the month, which was the nineteenth year of king Nebuchadnezzar, king of Babylon, came Nebuzaradan the captain of the guard, a servant of the …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Johanan the son of Kareah, and Seraiah the son of Tanhumeth the ______, and Jaazaniah the son of the Maacathite, they and their men.” (25:23); “… captivity of Jehoiachin king of Judah, in the twelfth month, on the ______ and twentieth day of the month, that Evilmerodach king of Babylon, in the …” (25:27, WEB) Hard
Answer: Netophathite; seven. The first passage reads “… Johanan the son of Kareah, and Seraiah the son of Tanhumeth the Netophathite, and Jaazaniah the son of the Maacathite, they and their men.”; the second reads “… captivity of Jehoiachin king of Judah, in the twelfth month, on the seven and twentieth day of the month, that Evilmerodach king of Babylon, in the …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The captain of the guard took Seraiah the chief priest, and ______ the second priest, and the three keepers of the threshold:” (25:18); “The king of Babylon struck them, and put them to ______ at Riblah in the land of Hamath. So Judah was carried away captive out of …” (25:21, WEB) Hard
A) Zephaniah; death B) Seth; death C) death; Zephaniah D) Zephaniah; sacrifice
Answer: A. Zephaniah; death. Verse 18 reads “The captain of the guard took Seraiah the chief priest, and Zephaniah the second priest, and the three keepers of the threshold:”; verse 21 reads “The king of Babylon struck them, and put them to death at Riblah in the land of Hamath. So Judah was carried away captive out of …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… the famine was sore in the city, so that there was no ______ for the people of the land.” (25:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: bread. The cited words are “… the famine was sore in the city, so that there was no bread for the people of the land.”
